[1;38;5;78mThe three blind
spots, and why two
of them are this
wave's own doing[0m

The census has
exactly one rot
check, at :585-602,
and it tests
EXISTENCE: a ledger
row naming a file
that is no longer on
disk is an error.
That catches
deletion. It cannot
catch the three
shapes that actually
rot a price column.

[38;5;59m• [0mSTALE PRICE. No
  content hash, no
  date arithmetic,
  no re-measurement.
  pds-record-
  parity.test.sh
  will print
  CPU=4.45s after
  someone doubles
  the harness's
  work, forever, and
  nothing reds.
[38;5;59m• [0mSTALE DISPOSITION
  EVIDENCE. Five
  rows cite "by run
  2026-08-03: <cmd>
  rc=N". The census
  never re-takes any
  of them. A
  disposition
  survives its own
  repair.
[38;5;59m• [0mORPHANED
  DISPOSITION. Read
  the cond at :519-
  540: legA=yes AND
  legB=true short-
  circuits to
  THROUGH and the
  ledger is NEVER
  consulted. A
  disposition row
  for a now-THROUGH
  instrument is
  silently unread -
  not an error, not
  a warning, not
  printed.

[38;5;69mTHE COUPLING THAT
MAKES THIS THE SPINE
RATHER THAN A
TANGENT: two of the
three deferred
slices the wish
orders will make the
census lie the day
they land. pds-w44-
hetzner-offline-door
puts --selftest-
offline through a
rider plus
ELIXIR_TEST_ONLY_PAT
HS - legA/legB then
compute THROUGH and
the ENVIRONMENT row
at :128 becomes an
unflagged orphan
asserting a refusal
that is no longer
the disposition.
pds-w44-charter-
sweep-adjudication
takes the sweep from
rc=1 to rc=0 - and
CONTENT-RED,
evidenced by a run
nobody re-takes,
outlives the red it
names. Price
provenance is a
PREREQUISITE for the
wish's own item 2,
not an addition to
it.[0m

[1;38;5;78mThe direction[0m

The epic's law has
walked one rung per
wave. A receipt lies
when the value it
emits does not
descend from the
write return. A
class lies when it
does not descend
from its
substitution (wave
42). A gate lies
when its green costs
nothing to produce
(wave 43). Wave 45's
sentence is the one
the new instrument
earns:

[38;5;69mA PRICE LIES WHEN IT
DOES NOT DESCEND
FROM A METER THE
INSTRUMENT ITSELF
DROVE - AND A
DISPOSITION LIES
WHEN ITS EVIDENCE
NAMES A RUN THE
INSTRUMENT NEVER RE-
TAKES.[0m

What the finished
wave looks like:
./scripts/pds-door-
census.sh still
exits 0 on main and
still costs seconds 
-
it is a door, and
its own 3.32 s row
is its own gate
cost, so any design
that prices it out
of its own door is
disqualified before
it is written. But
every number it
prints is now either
taken by a meter it
drove in THIS run,
or a cached
measurement carrying
the content hash of
the instrument it
prices, so that a
changed instrument
turns the row PRICE-
STALE and reds. A --
measure verb takes
the measurement and
emits the exact
ledger row, so re-
pricing is one
command rather than
a hand ritual - the
number descends from
a meter the
instrument drove,
and anyone can re-
take it.
Dispositions whose
evidence is a RUN
get re-run under an
opt-in, threshold-
printed tier, so
CONTENT-RED cannot
survive its own
repair. And an
orphaned ledger row
is an ERROR, because
that is the exact
shape both of this
wave's own slices
create.

Around that spine,
the debts get paid
at the new base.
Priority order, and
the reasoning behind
it:

[1;38;5;78mThe direction debate[0m

Three directions
were developed far
enough to cost
something, then
argued against each
other.

[1;38;5;59mRival A - pay the
debts, period[0m

Do exactly the
wish's items 1
through 5: three
deferred slices, two
owed reviews, the
lv_verdict half, the
criterion lesson. Do
not touch the census
-
it exits 0, wave 44
declared it done,
and reopening it is
a fifth wave of
arguing about a
column. This is the
disciplined reading
of the brief and it
has a real claim on
the wave.

WHY IT LOSES: it
hands the wave a
false floor. Two of
its own three slices
silently corrupt the
instrument, so "pay
the debts"
manufactures the
next wave's finding.
And it violates the
mandate the user
restates every
single wave -
DERIVE, NEVER
TRANSCRIBE,
INCLUDING FROM THIS
BRIEF - by
inheriting "the
price column is an
instrument now" as a
premise instead of
running it. I ran
it. It is half an
instrument, and the
half that is prose
is the half the
column is named
after.

[1;38;5;59mRival B - measure
every price, every
run[0m

The pure fix: no
cache, no ledger, no
staleness possible.
The census drives
the meter for every
row on every
invocation and the
price column is a
measurement by
construction.

WHY IT LOSES, on the
epic's own evidence:
D648 proved wall is
not a property of a
door on a shared
host - a fixed
workload's wall
spanned 5.8x at
constant load while
its user CPU spanned
27 percent. A re-
measuring census
becomes the most
expensive program in
its own denominator:
40.33 s for pds-
ledger-
census_test.sh
alone, plus the
receipt census whose
expensive arm D646
showed conceals ~140
s minimum from any
outer meter. It
would also have to
price ITSELF - a
fixpoint. Worst of
all it converts a 4
s door into an
ungateable one,
killing the census's
own THROUGH row,
which is the single
most self-defeating
outcome available to
this wave. The
honest design is not
"always measure" but
"never assert a
measurement you
cannot re-take, and
know when it
expired."

[1;38;5;59mRival C - spend the
column, gate the
cheap doors[0m

Take the census
output at face value
and do the obvious
thing the wish's
title demands -
SPEND IT. Put every
affordable row
through the required
gate: hetzner
offline at 2.15 s,
pds-record-
parity.test.sh at
4.45 s, pds-scratch-
target_test.sh at
8.91 s. Maximum
visible progress,
and it is exactly
what four waves of
arguing were FOR.

WHY IT LOSES AS A
WHOLE DIRECTION, and
why it is absorbed
as a part: gating on
unverified prices is
D633's sin at one
remove. pds-scratch-
target_test.sh's own
shipped row says
"Hermeticity on a
runner WITHOUT local
Postgres is unproven
here" - gating it is
a day-one red on a
foreign runner, the
identical trap wave
44 documented for
sheet-grid-js.yml.
Rival C survives as
exactly ONE new
door: the one whose
price and green I
measured myself
today.

[1;38;5;59mWhy the winner won[0m

The spine plus the
debts, with hetzner-
offline as the one
new door, is the
only direction in
which the wish's own
item 2 does not
manufacture the next
wave's finding. It
applies the epic's
law to the epic's
newest artifact,
which is what every
wave of this epic
has actually been
about. And it
converts "spend the
column" from a
slogan into
something that is
safe to do more than
once.

[1;38;5;59mThe sharpest attack
on the winner, and
how the wave absorbs
it[0m

[38;5;69mTHE ATTACK: "You are
reopening a slice
that shipped 11/12
with run evidence
and rc=0 on main, on
a distinction -
'measured' versus
'cached with
provenance' - that
is philosophy, not a
defect. Nobody is
harmed by a three-
day-old CPU figure.
This is the fifth
consecutive wave
arguing about a
column, which is
precisely what wave
44 ended."[0m

That attack has real
force, and the wave
absorbs it by
refusing to argue
and making it a RUN.
The spine ships only
if the verify round
can demonstrate, by
mutation against a
real tree, a case
where the shipped
census prints a
number or a class
the tree
contradicts. I
already hold one
candidate - the
hetzner row's wrong-
arm evidence - and
this wave's own
slices manufacture
two more. IF THE
VERIFIERS CANNOT
MAKE THE SHIPPED
CENSUS PRINT A
FALSEHOOD AGAINST A
REAL TREE, THE SPINE
IS CUT AND THIS WAVE
BECOMES RIVAL A.
That is the fail-
before condition,
declared before the
fleet flies, and it
is the honest
version of "derive,
never transcribe"
turned on my own
direction.

Second-order
absorption, as a
criterion rather
than a hope: the
design must not
balloon the census's
own price. A content
hash per row is one
shasum across 20
files -
milliseconds. Re-
running cheap
dispositions is opt-
in and threshold-
printed, never on --
check's default
path.

[1;38;5;78mThe survey plan[0m

Fifteen Opus

[1;38;5;78mTHE TWO FINDINGS
REVIEW ADDED, AND
THE RULES THEY BUY[0m

PDS-D664 — A
REPAIRED PREDICATE
CARRIES ITS OLD
DEFECT FOR EXACTLY
ONE LINE. arg_span
replaced the three-
line proximity
window with a real
paren-balance walk,
and the three fraud
shapes flipped as
designed. It still
returned the whole
CLOSING LINE, so
"System.cmd(...) ;
File.regular?(@fraud
)" — a token named
after the closing
paren, on the call's
own line — still
read as argument-
list membership:
bound, never
executed, THROUGH
with a price. One
line of the old
window living inside
the new predicate,
past a 13-arm green
selftest. THE RULE:
when you replace a
window with a
boundary, the
boundary is where
the window's last
residue hides — test
the token that sits
just OUTSIDE the new
edge, not only the
ones that sat inside
the old one.

PDS-D665 — BOTH LEGS
OR NEITHER APPLIES
TO EVIDENCE STRINGS,
NOT ONLY TO DECLARED
PATH SETS. The wish
states the both-legs
law for the hetzner
slice's
ELIXIR_TEST_ONLY_PAT
HS entry. It bit
somewhere nobody
named: the sweep's
own fix makes --
selftest rc=0, which
turns the door
census row whose
evidence quotes "--
selftest rc=1" into
a stale receipt the
moment it merges —
on the instrument
whose entire subject
is receipts that
lie. The builder was
fenced out of the
census and correctly
recorded the
correction in two
durable venues
rather than reaching
across the fence.
Review co-landed the
one-line row edit on
the SAME branch: a
behaviour and the
receipt describing
it merge together or
not at all. A fence
that splits those
two has to be closed
by whoever holds
both, and the fix
belongs on the
branch that causes
the drift.

[1;38;5;78mGRADE — A[0m

WHAT EARNS IT. The
wave paid all three
items the wish named
and paid them in the
order the
dependencies allow.
The second-review
debt on #9377 is now
discharged BY THE
MERGING ARTIFACT
rather than by a
reviewer's hand
mutation — that was
the live gap on
merged code, and it
is the difference
between "someone
proved it once" and
"the repo proves it
every run". The
ladder slice is the
wave's best work: it
recognised that its
own headline number
is evidence of
nothing, said so in
the printed output,
and shipped the only
discriminator that
exists as a selftest
case verified in
both directions. The
sweep slice
reproduced its fail-
open with the
CORRECT oracle after
the criterion named
the wrong one, and
closed it with the
discriminator that
was already on the
wire. Four of five
slices refuted
something in their
own dispatch by run
— the eleventh,
twelfth and
thirteenth
consecutive
refutations — and
each wrote the
correction into the
artifact instead of
quietly satisfying a
figure that does not
exist.

WHAT KEEPS IT OFF
A+. Two of the five
needed a reviewer
fix to a predicate
that had just been
tightened, and in
both cases the
defect was one step
past where the
builder stopped
looking: the first
occurrence rather
than the only
occurrence, the
closing line rather
than the closing
paren. Both slices
had green, honest,
non-vacuous
selftests through
the defect. The wave
also left three real
UNPROVEN arms
shipping green by
print rather than by
mutation — the
ladder's :stale
filter, arg_span's
"every call opening
on the line" clause,
and its 40-line
bound — each filed,
none closed, and
each is exactly the
shape this epic
exists to refuse.
And every price and
timing in this wave
is LOCAL: no PDS
door has yet run on
a foreign runner, so
the price column
remains an
instrument
calibrated against
one host.

NOT A CRITICISM, ON
THE RECORD: the
wish's item 4 (the
unbuilt "?"
variable-bound
decline) was RULED
OUT with a reason,
not quietly dropped
— PDS-D661 — and the
ladder slice printed
declined_live=0 as a
BOUND on the
sentence instead,
derived THROUGH
lvs_joinable?/1 so
the number cannot
drift from the
predicate it
describes. Ruling it
out was the right
half of "finish it
or rule it out".
